# QA test matrix

Status counts include planned acceptance contracts. A passing layout case checks only the destination reached, HTTP status, root overflow and page errors. Export header, PDF signature, product-count, service and end-to-end assertions have separate rows. Individual controls retain open acceptance rows until their full contract is exercised.

| FAIL | 0 |
| BLOCKED | 9 |
| NOT RUN | 765 |
| NOT IMPLEMENTED | 5 |
